## `AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Pull-Out-Anchor Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Paint Spectrophotometer Delta-E Match & Door Lock Cylinder QC
- **Intro:** Pull-Out-Anchor Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Paint Spectrophotometer Delta-E Match & Door Lock Cylinder QC — Pre-Operational Environmental Verification: Temperature: 23°C ± 2°C; Relative Humidity: 50% ± 10%; Illuminance: 500 lux ± 50 lux; Vibration amplitude ≤ 0.05 mm at 10 Hz.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 23°C ± 2°C; Relative Humidity: 50% ± 10%; Illuminance: 500 lux ± 50 lux; Vibration amplitude ≤ 0.05 mm at 10 Hz.
- **Standards:** `ANSI/IES LM-58-2020 (Spectrophotometric measurements)`, `ISO 11664-4:2019`, `ISO 9001:2015`, `ANSI/BHMA A156.2-2017 (Bored and preassembled locks and latches)`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-4752-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)

- **Role:** Airless-Plunger Mechanic
